    document.addEventListener('readystatechange', function() {
    	if (document.readyState != "interactive" || document.querySelector('#WrapperWithRightBlock') == null) {
    		return;
    	}
    	
    	var appContainer = document.querySelector('#WrapperWithRightBlock #mainWrapper .table-row #app_container');
    	var productsTable = appContainer.querySelector('.products_table');
    	
    	// правая координата таблицы
		var tableContentRightOffset = (productsTable || appContainer).getBoundingClientRect().right;
		
		// правая колонка с баннерами
		var rightColumn = document.querySelector('#WrapperWithRightBlock .right_right_column');
		
		// левая координата правой колонки
		var rightColumnLeftOffset = rightColumn.getBoundingClientRect().left;
		
		// получение значения padding-left из правой колонки
		var paddingLeft = parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(rightColumn, null).getPropertyValue('padding-left').replace(/\D/g, ''));
		
		// разница между таблицей и правой колонкой
		var offset = Math.ceil(tableContentRightOffset - rightColumnLeftOffset);
		
		if (offset > 0) {
			rightColumn.style.paddingLeft = (offset + paddingLeft) + 'px';
		}

		window.setTimeout(function() {
			var style = document.createElement('style');
			var table = document.querySelector('#content_column .table');
			
			// считаем ширину левой колонки и ширину таблицы, в яндекс-браузере иначе неправильно считается ширина
			var appContainer = table.querySelector('#app_container');
			var productsTable = appContainer.querySelector('.products_table');
			var appContainerWidth;
			if (productsTable != null) {
				appContainerWidth = Math.floor(productsTable.getBoundingClientRect().right - appContainer.getBoundingClientRect().left);
			} else {
				appContainerWidth = appContainer.offsetWidth;
			}
			var widthLeftCenterColumn = table.querySelector('.left_column').offsetWidth + appContainerWidth + 360;
			
			style.innerText = '  @media screen and (max-width: ' + widthLeftCenterColumn + 'px) {'
			+'      #WrapperWithRightBlock #mainWrapper {'
			+'          flex-grow: 1;'
			+'          flex-basis: 0;'
			+'      }'
			+''
			+'      .right_right_column, .footer-next {'
			+'          display: none !important;'
			+'      }'
			+'  }';
			
			document.body.appendChild(style);
		}, 0);
	});

<script type="text/javascript">
        
            window.clientAppVersion = "20191014231200385"
        
        try {
            var ErrorHandler = {

                XMLHttpFactories : [
                    function () {return new XMLHttpRequest()},
                    function () {return new ActiveXObject("Msxml2.XMLHTTP")},
                    function () {return new ActiveXObject("Msxml3.XMLHTTP")},
                    function () {return new ActiveXObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP")}
                ],

                createXMLHTTPObject : function() {
                    var xmlhttp = false;
                    for (var i = 0; i < this.XMLHttpFactories.length; i++) {
                        try {
                            xmlhttp = this.XMLHttpFactories[i]();
                        } catch(e) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    return xmlhttp;
                },

                sendJSONRequest : function(url, callback, postData) {
                    try {
                        var req = this.createXMLHTTPObject();
                        if (!req) return;
                        var method = 'POST';
                        req.open(method,url,true);
                        if (postData) {
                            req.setRequestHeader('Content-type','text/plain');
                        }
                        req.onreadystatechange = function () {
                            if (req.readyState != 4) return;
                            if (req.status != 200 && req.status != 304) {
                                return;
                            }
                            callback(req);
                        }
                        if (req.readyState == 4) return;
                        req.send(postData);
                    } catch(e) {
                        //noop
                    }
                },

                attachErrorhandler : function(callbackFunction) {
                    try {
                        if (window.addEventListener) {
                            window.addEventListener('error', function(err) {
                                callbackFunction(err);
                            });
                        } else if (window.attachEvent) {
                            window.attachEvent('onerror', function(msg, url, line) {
                                callbackFunction({
                                    'message' : msg,
                                    'filename' : url,
                                    'lineno' : line,
                                    'colno' : null
                                });
                            });
                        }
                    } catch(e) {
                        //noop
                    }
                },

                errorExtension: []

                };
                
                // see also util/ErrorHandler.js. In case of changes change logic there as well.
                var isUnidentifiableError = function(message, line, column, error, url) {
                    return (message === 'Script error.' && line === 0 && column === 0 && error == null) || (url === 'https://mc.yandex.ru/metrika/watch.js');
                };

                var isExternalScriptError = function (message, pageUrl, url) {
                    return (/(Failed to execute '\w+' on 'Node':.*is not a child of this node\.$)/i.test(message) || (message.indexOf('Mismatched anonymous define() module') !== -1) || (pageUrl === url));
                };

                ErrorHandler.attachErrorhandler(function(error) {
                    try {
                        var message = error.message + ', Page Url: ' + window.location.href + ', Url:' + error.filename + ', Line:' + error.lineno + ', Column:' + error.colno + ', Stack:' + (error.error && error.error.stack ? error.error.stack.toString() : '') + ', User agent:' + navigator.userAgent;
                        if (/^(127|10)\.0\.\d{1,3}\.\d{1,3}(:\d+)?$/g.test(window.location.hostname)) {
                            return;
                        }
                        if (isUnidentifiableError(error.message, error.lineno, error.colno, error.error, error.filename)) {
                            // possible error from external script that we cannot identify due to CORS restrictions
                            message = 'UNIDENTIFIABLE_SCRIPT_ERROR ' + message;
                        }
                        if (ErrorHandler.errorExtension.length > 0){
                            message += '\r\n' + 'jsonText: ' + JSON.stringify(ErrorHandler.errorExtension);
                        }

                        if (isExternalScriptError(error.message, window.location.href, error.filename)) {
                            var scriptTags = [].slice.call(document.getElementsByTagName('script')).map(function (s) {
                                return s.outerHTML.substring(0, 300);
                            });
                            message += ', Script tags: \r\n' + JSON.stringify(scriptTags.join('\n===========================\n'), null, '\t').replace(/\\n/g, "\n");
                        }

                        if (window.clientAppVersion) {
                            message += '\r\n' + 'clientAppVersion=' + window.clientAppVersion;
                        }

                        ErrorHandler.sendJSONRequest('/lundaonline/common/jserrors', function(response) {}, message);
                    } catch(e) {
                        //noop
                    }
                });
        } catch(e) {
            //noop
        }
    </script>
